* `2Sa 1:23` "Saul and Jonathan were lovely and pleasant in their lives, and in their death they were not divided. They were swifter than *_eagles_*, they were stronger than lions.
* `Pro 30:17` The eye that mocks at its father and despises to obey its mother, the ravens of the valley shall pick it out, and the young *_eagles_* shall eat it.
* `Isa 40:31` But those who wait upon the LORD shall renew their strength. They shall mount up with wings like *_eagles_*. They shall run and not be weary. They shall walk and not faint.
* `Jer 4:13` "Behold, he shall come up as clouds, and his chariots shall be as a whirlwind. His horses are swifter than *_eagles_*. Woe to us! For we are laid waste."
* `Lam 4:19` [QOF] Our persecutors are swifter than the *_eagles_* of heaven. They pursued us upon the mountains. They laid wait for us in the wilderness.
* `Dan 4:33` The same hour, the thing was fulfilled upon Nebuchadnezzar. He was driven from men and ate grass as oxen, and his body was wet with the dew of heaven, until his hairs were grown like *_eagles_*' feathers and his nails like birds' claws.
* `Dan 7:4` The first was like a lion and had *_eagles_*' wings. I beheld until its wings were plucked, and it was lifted up from the earth and made to stand upon the feet as a man, and a man's heart was given to it.
* `Mth 24:28` For wherever the carcass is, there the *_eagles_* will be collected.
* `Luk 17:37` And they answered and said to him, "Where Lord?" And he said to them, "Wherever the body is, there the *_eagles_* will be collected."

* `Rom 14:19` Let us therefore follow after the things which make for peace and things with which one may *_edify_* another.
* `1Co 10:23` "All things are lawful for me," but all things are not expedient. "All things are lawful for me," but all things do not *_edify_*.
* `1Th 5:11` Therefore, comfort yourselves together and *_edify_* one another, even as you also do.

_3 verses containing EDIFY_

== EDIFYING

* `1Co 14:5` I wish that you all spoke in languages, but more so that you prophesied. For greater is he who prophesies than he who speaks in languages, unless he interprets, so that the church may receive *_edifying_*.
* `1Co 14:12` Even so you, because you are zealous of spiritual gifts, seek so that you may excel to the *_edifying_* of the church.
* `Eph 4:12` For the perfecting of the saints, for the work of the ministry, for the *_edifying_* of the body of Christ,
* `Eph 4:16` From whom the whole body properly joined together and united by that which every joint supplies, according to the effectual working in the measure of every part, makes increase of the body to the *_edifying_* of itself in love.
* `Eph 4:29` Let no corrupt communication proceed out of your mouth, but that which is good to the use of *_edifying_* so that it may minister grace to the hearers.
* `1Ti 1:4` Neither give heed to fables and endless genealogies, which minister questions rather than godly *_edifying_* which is in faith.

* `Jms 5:16` Confess your sins to one another and pray for one another so that you may be healed. The *_effectual_* fervent prayer of the righteous man avails much.

== EFFECTUALLY

_1 verse containing EFFEMINATE_

== EGG

* `Job 6:6` Can that which is unsavory be eaten without salt? Or is there any taste in the white of an *_egg_*?
* `Luk 11:12` Or if he asks for an *_egg_*, will he offer him a scorpion?

_2 verses containing EGG_

== EGGS

* `Deu 22:6` "If a bird's nest happens to be before you in the way on any tree or on the ground, whether with young ones or *_eggs_*, and the mother sitting upon the young or upon the *_eggs_*, you shall not take the mother with the young.
* `Job 39:14` Which leaves her *_eggs_* in the earth, warms them in the dust,
* `Isa 10:14` And my hand has found the riches of the people like a nest. And like one gathering *_eggs_* that are left, I have gathered all the earth, and there was no one who moved the wing, opened the mouth, or peeped."
* `Isa 59:5` They hatch adders' *_eggs_* and weave the spider's web. He who eats their *_eggs_* dies, and that which is crushed breaks out into a viper.
* `Jer 17:11` As the partridge sits on *_eggs_* and does not hatch them, so he who gets riches, and not by right, shall leave them in the midst of his days, and at his end shall be a fool."
